        Current plan (markdown):
        # Implementation Plan: `megaplan cloud` — Sprint 1 (provider-agnostic Railway runner)

## Overview

Goal: introduce a new `megaplan/cloud/` subpackage that generalises the working-but-hardcoded Railway deployment at `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/` into a reusable, spec-driven cloud runner exposed through the existing `megaplan` CLI. Sprint 1 ships only the Railway provider; the abstraction is built so sprint 2 can drop in fly/ssh/local providers without churn.

Repo shape (relevant): the `megaplan` package uses argparse subcommands wired in `megaplan/cli.py` (`build_parser`, `cli.py:826`), with multi-action subcommands defined per-module via `build_<X>_parser(subparsers)` + `run_<X>_cli(root, args)` (see `megaplan/chain.py:528` and dispatch at `megaplan/cli.py:1133-1138`). Package data is loaded with `importlib.resources.files("megaplan")` (`megaplan/cli.py:600`). Hatchling packages the whole `megaplan/` tree, so any new resource files (Dockerfile, entrypoint, wrappers, templates) ship automatically. PyYAML is already a runtime dependency (`pyproject.toml:22`). Existing `.gitignore` already lists `__pycache__/`, `.megaplan/`, `dist/`, `*.egg-info/`, `activity-summary-*.md` (no rule for `_reference/` yet — Step 0 adds one).

Hard contracts the plan must respect (verified against the codebase, fixing prior-iteration mistakes):
- **Remote command name**: the deployed image installs `megaplan` globally (reigh `Dockerfile:61-63`); README operations use bare `megaplan` inside `/workspace/<repo>`. The cloud layer must use `megaplan ...`, NOT `./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan` — there is no `.venv` in the container.
- **Config namespace**: `megaplan config set` only accepts `agents.<step>` keys, where `<step>` is validated against `DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING` (`megaplan/cli.py:758-777`, `megaplan/types.py:296-309`). There is no `phase_agent.*` namespace.
- **`next_step` translation**: `megaplan/auto.py:128-136` (`_phase_command`) is the canonical translator and adds `--confirm-destructive --user-approved` for `execute`. `cloud resume` must reuse this helper.
- **Active-plan resolution**: `handle_status` (`cli.py:353-367`) calls `load_plan(root, args.plan)` which already auto-resolves when `args.plan is None`. The cloud layer passes `--plan` through if the user provided one and omits it otherwise — no client-side discovery.
- **Railway service/session targeting**: existing reigh workflow uses `railway --service agent variables --set ...` and `railway ssh --session agent`. The Railway provider threads service + session identity through every CLI call.
- **Workspace path is data, not magic**: reigh hardcodes `/workspace/reigh-app` in five places (`safe.directory`, `REPO_DIR`, tmux `-c`, README `cd` invocations). The spec MUST carry this path as a first-class field; templates and remote commands MUST read it from there.
- **Volume teardown is part of `destroy`**: reigh README defines full teardown as `railway down` + `railway volume delete <volume>`. `Provider.destroy(*, volume=None)` and `RailwayProvider` MUST honor a configured volume name.
- **No dead knobs**: `railway.project` is wired (called once at deploy via `railway link --project <project>` when set) — not added speculatively.

Other constraints:
- Sprint 1 must be independently shippable.
- Idempotency contract preserved (clone-if-missing, codex config always overwritten, megaplan always upgraded).
- Codex `sandbox_mode = "danger-full-access"` retained with explanatory comment.
- `MEGAPLAN_REF` pin surfaced as `megaplan.ref` (default `main`).
- Adding `cloud` must not break any existing command.
- Reigh parity: a `cloud.yaml` matching today's reigh env vars must reproduce the existing deployment.

## Phase 1: Reconcile Source & Scaffold Subpackage

### Step 0: Vendor reigh source as reference, with ignore + build exclusion (`megaplan/cloud/_reference/`, `.gitignore`, `pyproject.toml`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Verify** the executor can read `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/`. If readable, proceed; if sandboxed, copy the entire directory tree into `megaplan/cloud/_reference/` so subsequent steps have ground truth.
2. **Append** the line `megaplan/cloud/_reference/` to `.gitignore` (the file currently has 9 lines; add at the bottom). This prevents the snapshot from being committed if the vendoring fallback is used.
3. **Exclude** the snapshot from packaged artifacts: add `[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel.exclude] packages = ["megaplan/cloud/_reference"]` (or the equivalent `exclude = ["megaplan/cloud/_reference/**"]`) to `pyproject.toml`. Verify by inspecting the built wheel — `_reference/` must NOT appear.
4. **Document** the snapshot status in `megaplan/cloud/_reference/README.md` (one paragraph: "frozen reference from reigh-megaplan-dev as of <date>; do not edit; not shipped").

### Step 1: Read every file in the reigh deployment
**Scope:** Small
1. **Read** every file in `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/` (or the vendored mirror): `Dockerfile`, `entrypoint.sh`, `healthserver.py`, `railway.toml`, `chain.sh`, `chain.yaml.example`, `README.md`, `BRIEFING_RULES.md`, every file under `ideas/`, every top-level `megaplan-idea*.txt`, plus anything else present.
2. **Catalogue** every hardcoded value (repo URL, **workspace path** (`/workspace/reigh-app`), model, reasoning, email, agent routing, robustness, **persistent volume name** (`agent-volume`)) and every environment variable referenced by `entrypoint.sh`.
3. **Confirm** the exact volume name reigh uses (per README: `agent-volume`) and the Railway service/session names (`agent`/`agent`) — these become defaults in the `railway:` block of `cloud.yaml`.
4. **Note** the exact mp-run / mp-supervise / mp-heartbeat heredoc bodies — these become standalone files in step 3.
5. **Identify** the exact `claude` CLI auth invocation. Test what the installed `claude` CLI exposes for non-interactive auth. Per user note #3: if no non-interactive path exists, the rendered entrypoint emits a warning and continues (do NOT block deploy).

### Step 2: Scaffold the subpackage layout (`megaplan/cloud/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** the directory tree:
   ```
   megaplan/cloud/
     __init__.py
     cli.py              # build_cloud_parser + run_cloud_cli
     spec.py             # cloud.yaml dataclasses + loader/validator
     template.py         # entrypoint renderer
     providers/
       __init__.py
       base.py           # Provider ABC
       railway.py        # Railway implementation
     templates/
       Dockerfile
       entrypoint.sh.tmpl
       healthserver.py
       railway.toml.tmpl
       cloud.yaml.tmpl
     wrappers/
       mp-run
       mp-supervise
       mp-heartbeat
   ```
2. **Confirm** hatchling packages the non-`.py` resources by default. If a `[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel.force-include]` block is needed, add it (sharing the `pyproject.toml` change with Step 0's exclude block).
3. **Verify** by building the wheel locally (`./.venv/bin/python -m build --wheel`) and inspecting wheel contents — `templates/`, `wrappers/` present; `_reference/` absent.

### Step 3: Port resources verbatim, then parameterise (`megaplan/cloud/templates/`, `megaplan/cloud/wrappers/`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Copy** `Dockerfile`, `healthserver.py`, and `railway.toml` from reigh into `megaplan/cloud/templates/` with no behavioural changes. The Dockerfile bakes the toolchain only; megaplan is installed globally via `pip install` so `megaplan` resolves on the container PATH.
2. **Copy** `entrypoint.sh` to `megaplan/cloud/templates/entrypoint.sh.tmpl` and replace every hardcoded value with a Python `string.Template` placeholder. The complete placeholder list:
   - `${REPO_URL}`, `${REPO_BRANCH}`
   - `${WORKSPACE_PATH}` — used in 5 places: git `safe.directory`, `REPO_DIR=`, the `git clone` destination, the tmux `agent` session `-c <path>`, and any other `cd` reference. Wherever the original entrypoint had `/workspace/reigh-app`, the template has `${WORKSPACE_PATH}`.
   - `${CODEX_MODEL}`, `${CODEX_REASONING}`, `${CODEX_EMAIL}`
   - `${MEGAPLAN_REF}`, `${ROBUSTNESS}`, `${MODE}`
   - `${IDEA_FILE}`, `${CHAIN_SPEC}`
   - `${AGENT_ROUTING_BLOCK}` (multi-line `megaplan config set agents.<step> <agent>` lines)
   - `${CLAUDE_AUTH_BLOCK}` (the gated auth-or-warn shell block)
   - Plus any others discovered in Step 1.
   Keep the explanatory comment for `sandbox_mode = "danger-full-access"`.
3. **Extract** the mp-run / mp-supervise / mp-heartbeat heredocs into standalone files under `megaplan/cloud/wrappers/`. Add a `COPY megaplan/cloud/wrappers/* /usr/local/bin/` (and `chmod +x`) step to the Dockerfile.
4. **Render** the Claude auth block. If the discovered command is `<cmd>`:
   ```
   if [ -n "${ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:-}" ]; then
       <cmd> || echo "[entrypoint] WARNING: claude auth failed; continuing"
   else
       echo "[entrypoint] ANTHROPIC_API_KEY not set; skipping claude auth"
   fi
   ```
   If no non-interactive path exists, the block is a single warning `echo` — never `exit 1`.
5. **Add** `cloud.yaml.tmpl` with sensible defaults filled in (including `repo.workspace: /workspace/app`, `railway.service: agent`, `railway.session: agent`, `resources.volume: agent-volume`) and inline comments explaining each section.

### Step 4: Spec loader (`megaplan/cloud/spec.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Define** dataclasses. The `repo` block now carries the `workspace` path (the field that addresses the workspace-path flags); `resources.volume` is wired in step 7's `destroy`; `railway.project` is wired in step 7's `deploy`:
   ```python
   @dataclass
   class RepoSpec:
       url: str
       branch: str = "main"
       workspace: str = "/workspace/app"   # absolute container path: clone dest, tmux cwd, README cd target
   @dataclass
   class CodexSpec: model: str = "gpt-5.4"; reasoning: str = "high"
   @dataclass
   class AutoSpec: idea_file: str; robustness: str = "standard"
   @dataclass
   class ChainSubSpec: spec: str
   @dataclass
   class ResourcesSpec:
       volume: str | None = None           # Railway volume name; if set, `cloud destroy` deletes it after `down`
       port: int = 8080
   @dataclass
   class MegaplanSpec: ref: str = "main"
   @dataclass
   class RailwaySpec:
       service: str = "agent"
       session: str = "agent"
       project: str | None = None          # if set, `cloud deploy` runs `railway link --project <project>` first
   @dataclass
   class CloudSpec:
       provider: str
       repo: RepoSpec
       agents: dict[str, str]              # keys validated against DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING + "default"
       codex: CodexSpec
       mode: str                           # "auto" | "chain" | "idle"
       megaplan: MegaplanSpec
       resources: ResourcesSpec
       secrets: list[str]
       auto: AutoSpec | None = None
       chain: ChainSubSpec | None = None
       railway: RailwaySpec | None = None  # required when provider == "railway"; loader fills with defaults
   ```
2. **Implement** `load_spec(path: Path) -> CloudSpec` — open, `yaml.safe_load`, validate, raise `CliError("invalid_spec", ...)` on every missing/malformed field. Mirror `megaplan/chain.py:71-100`.
3. **Validate** mode-specific requirements: `mode=auto` ⇒ `auto.idea_file` required; `mode=chain` ⇒ `chain.spec` required; `mode=idle` ⇒ neither required.
4. **Validate** `agents` keys: `default` permitted; every other key MUST be in `DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING` (imported from `megaplan.types`). Reject unknown keys with `CliError("invalid_spec", f"agents.{key} not in DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING: {sorted(DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING)}")`.
5. **Validate** every value in `agents` is in `KNOWN_AGENTS`.
6. **Validate** `provider`: only `"railway"` permitted in sprint 1; reject others with `CliError("invalid_spec", "sprint 2 will add: fly, ssh, local")`.
7. **Validate** `repo.workspace` is an absolute POSIX path (starts with `/`); reject otherwise.
8. **Fill** `RailwaySpec` with defaults if the `railway:` block is omitted, but always materialise the dataclass when `provider == "railway"`.

### Step 5: Template renderer (`megaplan/cloud/template.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `render_entrypoint(spec: CloudSpec) -> str` using `string.Template.safe_substitute`. Source the `.tmpl` from package resources via `importlib.resources.files("megaplan.cloud").joinpath("templates/entrypoint.sh.tmpl").read_text(...)`. The substitution map MUST include `WORKSPACE_PATH=spec.repo.workspace` so every reference in the template (safe.directory, REPO_DIR, clone dest, tmux `-c`) resolves to the spec value.
2. **Build** the agent-routing block. For each entry in `spec.agents`:
   - `default` → `megaplan config set agents.<step> <default_agent>` for every `<step>` in `DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING`.
   - Per-step keys → `megaplan config set agents.<step> <agent>` (overrides default for that step).
   Output is shell lines only — no `phase_agent.*`, no fabricated namespace.
3. **Implement** `render_railway_toml(spec)` and `materialize_deploy_dir(spec, dest: Path)` that copies the Dockerfile, healthserver, wrappers, and the rendered entrypoint + railway.toml into a deploy staging dir.
4. **Single source of truth**: define placeholder names as a module-level tuple `PLACEHOLDERS = ("REPO_URL", "REPO_BRANCH", "WORKSPACE_PATH", ...)` so tests iterate over them and assert "no placeholder remains unfilled".

## Phase 2: Provider Layer

### Step 6: Provider abstract base (`megaplan/cloud/providers/base.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Define** an `abc.ABC` `Provider`. Note the new `volume` parameter on `destroy` and the new `workspace` parameter that callers (cloud CLI) pass into status/exec helpers — provider implementations may use it to construct `cd <workspace> && ...` commands:
   ```python
   class Provider(ABC):
       def build(self, deploy_dir: Path) -> int: ...
       def deploy(self, deploy_dir: Path, *, secrets: dict[str, str]) -> int: ...
       def ssh_exec(self, command: str) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess: ...
       def attach(self) -> int: ...
       def logs(self, *, follow: bool = True) -> int: ...
       def status_payload(self, *, plan: str | None, workspace: str) -> dict: ...
       def down(self) -> int: ...
       def destroy(self, *, volume: str | None = None) -> int: ...
   ```
2. **Document** in the docstring that sprint 2 will add `init_plan(self, ...)` and that providers must remain stateless beyond CLI/credential discovery.
3. **Provide** `get_provider(name: str, spec: CloudSpec) -> Provider`. Static dict for sprint 1: `{"railway": RailwayProvider}`.

### Step 7: Railway provider (`megaplan/cloud/providers/railway.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Construct** with the `RailwaySpec` (`service`, `session`, optional `project`) and stash `repo.workspace` for later. Every method targets the right service explicitly.
2. **Implement** each ABC method as a thin wrapper around `railway` CLI:
   - `build`: `docker build -t <image-tag> <deploy_dir>`.
   - `deploy(deploy_dir, *, secrets)`:
     1. If `spec.railway.project is not None`: run `railway link --project <project>` once to bind the working dir.
     2. For each `(name, value)` in `secrets`: `railway variables --service <service> --set NAME=VALUE`.
     3. `railway up --service <service> --detach --ci` from the deploy dir.
   - `ssh_exec(cmd)`: `railway ssh --service <service> --session <session> -- <cmd>`.
   - `attach`: `railway ssh --service <service> --session <session>` (interactive).
   - `logs`: `railway logs --service <service>` (with `--follow` if requested).
   - `status_payload(plan, workspace)`: build remote command as `cd <workspace> && megaplan status` plus `--plan <plan>` if provided. Stream stdout, parse JSON, return dict. Bare `megaplan` (globally installed); no `.venv`, no `python -m`.
   - `down`: `railway down --service <service>`.
   - `destroy(*, volume)`:
     1. `railway down --service <service>`.
     2. If `volume is not None`: `railway volume delete <volume>` (and check exit code; surface non-zero as `CliError("provider_failed", stderr)` so the user sees the failure rather than silently leaving the volume around).
3. **Detect** the `railway` binary up front via `shutil.which("railway")`; if missing, raise `CliError("provider_unavailable", "Install: https://docs.railway.app/develop/cli")`.
4. **`deploy()` secrets**: source values from `os.environ` for each name in `spec.secrets`. If any are missing locally, raise `CliError("missing_secrets", f"Missing env vars: {missing}")` BEFORE making any railway calls.
5. **No retry/backoff** beyond what `railway` already does. Surface non-zero exits as `CliError("provider_failed", stderr)`.

## Phase 3: CLI Wiring

### Step 8: Cloud CLI module (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** `build_cloud_parser(subparsers)` modelled on `megaplan/chain.py:528-555`. Top-level `cloud` parser with subparsers: `init`, `build`, `deploy`, `status`, `attach`, `logs`, `exec`, `resume`, `down`, `destroy`. All accept an optional `--cloud-yaml PATH` (defaults to `<root>/cloud.yaml`).
2. **Implement** `run_cloud_cli(root: Path, args: argparse.Namespace) -> int` that dispatches to per-action functions.
3. **Per-action behaviours:**
   - **`init`** — copies `cloud.yaml.tmpl` to `<root>/cloud.yaml`. Refuses to overwrite without `--force`.
   - **`build`** — renders deploy dir, calls `provider.build(deploy_dir)`.
   - **`deploy`** — renders deploy dir, gathers secret values from env, calls `provider.deploy(...)`. The provider handles `railway link --project ...` when configured.
   - **`status`** — accepts optional `--plan NAME`; calls `provider.status_payload(plan=args.plan, workspace=spec.repo.workspace)` and prints the JSON. When `--plan` is omitted, the remote `megaplan status` is invoked with no `--plan` flag and `load_plan(root, None)` resolves the active plan server-side.
   - **`attach`** — calls `provider.attach()`. `--session NAME` overrides the spec default for this single invocation.
   - **`logs`** — calls `provider.logs(follow=True)`. `--no-follow` for one-shot.
   - **`exec "<cmd>"`** — calls `provider.ssh_exec(cmd)`. Streams stdout/stderr; returns the remote exit code. Does NOT auto-prepend `cd <workspace>` (the user's command is opaque); document that pattern in `docs/cloud.md`.
   - **`resume`** — accepts optional `--plan NAME`. Steps:
     1. Call `provider.status_payload(plan=args.plan, workspace=spec.repo.workspace)`; read `next_step`.
     2. Translate via `from megaplan.auto import _phase_command; argv = _phase_command(next_step)`. Guarantees the `execute` special case is honored.
     3. Build the remote command: `cd <workspace> && megaplan ` + shell-quoted `argv`, plus `--plan <plan>` if the user specified one.
     4. Hand to `provider.ssh_exec(...)`.
   - **`down`** — calls `provider.down()`.
   - **`destroy`** — interactive confirmation by default; `--yes` skips. On confirmation, calls `provider.destroy(volume=spec.resources.volume)`. The prompt message names the volume (or "no volume configured") so the user sees what's about to be deleted.
4. **Errors:** all `CliError`s flow through `_emit_error` (mirror `megaplan/chain.py:594-597`).

### Step 9: Wire into top-level CLI (`megaplan/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Register** the parser inside `build_parser()` next to the existing `build_chain_parser` registration (`megaplan/cli.py:997-998`):
   ```python
   from megaplan.cloud.cli import build_cloud_parser
   build_cloud_parser(subparsers)
   ```
2. **Dispatch** in `main()` next to the `chain` block (`megaplan/cli.py:1133-1138`):
   ```python
   if args.command == "cloud":
       from megaplan.cloud.cli import run_cloud_cli
       try:
           return run_cloud_cli(root, args)
       except CliError as error:
           return error_response(error, root=root)
   ```
3. **Do NOT** add `"cloud"` to `COMMAND_HANDLERS` — it has its own dispatch path.
4. **Keep** all imports lazy/scoped.

## Phase 4: Tests, Docs, Smoke Build

### Step 10: Unit tests for spec & renderer (`tests/test_cloud_spec.py`, `tests/test_cloud_template.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **`test_cloud_spec.py`** — happy-path load for each `mode`, plus rejections: missing `repo.url`, missing `repo.workspace` validation when set to a non-absolute string, unknown provider, `mode=auto` without `idea_file`, `mode=chain` without `chain.spec`, an `agents` key that isn't `default` AND isn't in `DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING` (assert error names the actual valid set), an unknown agent value, malformed `secrets` list, and (for `provider: railway`) a missing `railway:` block being filled with defaults (`service: agent`, `session: agent`, `project: None`).
2. **`test_cloud_template.py`** — render with a fully-populated spec. Assert: (a) every documented placeholder is replaced (iterate over `template.PLACEHOLDERS`); (b) no original reigh hardcoded string remains (`banodoco/reigh-app`, `gpt-5.4`, `[REDACTED]`, `/workspace/reigh-app`); (c) `sandbox_mode = "danger-full-access"` line is preserved; (d) Claude auth block present and gated on `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` with warn-not-fatal semantics; (e) the agent-routing block emits ONLY `megaplan config set agents.<step> <agent>` lines; (f) for a per-step override, exactly one `agents.<step>` line is emitted with the override agent; (g) **a non-default `repo.workspace` (e.g. `/workspace/foo`) flows into every `WORKSPACE_PATH` substitution site in the rendered entrypoint** (no `/workspace/app` literal remains).

### Step 11: Unit test for the Railway provider with mocked subprocess (`tests/test_cloud_railway.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Patch** `subprocess.run` (and `shutil.which`) and assert exact `railway ...` argv produced by each provider method.
2. **Cover** that every railway invocation includes `--service <service>` and `ssh_exec`/`attach` include `--session <session>`.
3. **Cover** `build`, `deploy` (per-secret upload sequence with `--service`, AND when `spec.railway.project` is set the test asserts `railway link --project <project>` is called exactly once before any `variables`/`up` calls), `down`, `ssh_exec`, `attach`, `logs`.
4. **`status_payload(plan=None, workspace="/workspace/foo")`** — assert remote command is `cd /workspace/foo && megaplan status` (no `--plan`, no `.venv`, no `python -m`). `status_payload(plan="P", workspace="/workspace/foo")` — assert remote command is `cd /workspace/foo && megaplan status --plan P`.
5. **`destroy(volume=None)`** — assert exactly one `railway down --service <service>` call, no volume call.
6. **`destroy(volume="agent-volume")`** — assert `railway down --service <service>` followed by `railway volume delete agent-volume`. Add a second test where the volume-delete call returns non-zero and assert it raises `CliError("provider_failed", ...)`.
7. **Cover** the missing-binary path: `shutil.which("railway") -> None` ⇒ `CliError("provider_unavailable", ...)`.
8. **Cover** the missing-secret path: spec lists secret `FOO` but `os.environ` doesn't have it → `CliError` with the missing names, BEFORE any railway calls.

### Step 12: Unit test for `cloud resume` translation (`tests/test_cloud_resume.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Mock** `provider.status_payload` to return `{"next_step": "execute", ...}`. Assert the cloud-resume action calls `provider.ssh_exec` with a remote command containing `cd <workspace> && megaplan execute --confirm-destructive --user-approved`.
2. **Cover** at least one non-execute step (`next_step == "review"`) to verify the bare-name path also works.
3. **Assert** that `--plan <name>` is appended when the user passed `--plan`, and omitted when they didn't.
4. **Assert** that the remote command's `cd` target is `spec.repo.workspace` (not a hardcoded path).

### Step 13: Smoke test for docker build (`tests/test_cloud_docker_build.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Skip** at module import if `shutil.which("docker") is None` with a clear `pytest.skip(...)` message.
2. **Materialise** a fully-rendered deploy dir into `tmp_path`, run `docker build -t megaplan-cloud-smoke .` against it. Fail on non-zero exit.
3. **Mark** with `@pytest.mark.slow` so CI can opt in/out.

### Step 14: User docs (`docs/cloud.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `docs/cloud.md`. Sections: quick start, `cloud.yaml` field reference (including `repo.workspace` with the explicit note that it is the container path used for cloning, tmux cwd, and remote `cd` targets; the `railway:` block including `service`/`session`/`project` and what each one does; the `resources.volume` field with explicit note that it controls `cloud destroy` volume deletion), env vars, secret-rotation pointer, cost-monitoring pointer (Railway dashboard link), the symptom→cause→fix table ported from the reigh README.
2. **Document** the resolved Claude non-interactive auth path (and the warn-not-fatal behaviour when no path exists).
3. **Document** `cloud destroy` semantics: it runs `railway down --service <service>` then, if `resources.volume` is set, `railway volume delete <volume>`. Warn that without `--yes` the prompt names the volume.
4. **Document** `cloud exec` does NOT auto-`cd` to the workspace; provide the recommended pattern `megaplan cloud exec "cd /workspace/app && megaplan ..."`.
5. **Document** the `railway` CLI install link and the failure mode if it's missing.
6. **Brief mention** that sprint 2 will add fly/ssh/local providers and `cloud init-plan`.

## Execution Order
1. Steps 0–1 first — vendoring (with ignore + build exclusion) and full read of the reigh dir feed every later decision.
2. Steps 2–3 lay down resources before any code references them.
3. Steps 4–5 (spec + renderer) before steps 6–7 (providers depend on spec dataclasses, including the new `repo.workspace` field).
4. Steps 8–9 (CLI wiring) after providers exist.
5. Tests (10–13) run alongside or after each implementation step they cover.
6. Docs (14) last so they reflect shipped behaviour.

## Validation Order
1. Run `pytest tests/test_cloud_spec.py tests/test_cloud_template.py tests/test_cloud_resume.py -x` (cheapest, no external deps).
2. Run `pytest tests/test_cloud_railway.py -x` (mocked subprocess).
3. Run the full suite: `./.venv/bin/python -m pytest -x` to confirm no regression.
4. Run `./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan cloud init` in a scratch dir; confirm `cloud.yaml` is written with `repo.workspace`, `railway.service`, `resources.volume` defaults.
5. Run `./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan cloud build` against the scratch `cloud.yaml`; confirm a Docker image is produced (skip if no docker).
6. Build the wheel (`./.venv/bin/python -m build --wheel`) and confirm `_reference/` is absent from the artifact while `templates/` and `wrappers/` are present.
7. Manual: `./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan cloud deploy` against a throwaway Railway project mirroring reigh's env vars; confirm container boots, codex auth succeeds, claude auth either succeeds or warns (never blocks), megaplan installs at the pinned ref, tmux sessions start in the configured workspace path, healthserver answers on `:8080`. `cloud status` returns parsed JSON; `cloud resume` on an `execute`-state plan dispatches `megaplan execute --confirm-destructive --user-approved` over SSH; `cloud destroy --yes` runs `railway down` then `railway volume delete <name>` and the volume is gone from the Railway dashboard.

  "recommendation": "ITERATE",
  "rationale": "Score still dropping productively (21.5 \u2192 7.5 \u2192 6.5), 16 flags resolved over 2 iterations, no reopened flags. The 7 open significant flags collapse to two concrete, mechanical fixes \u2014 no architectural tension. Per the tiebreaker rule: no fuzzy group has `addressed_then_reopened_count >= 2`, and no group has >=2 member flags (each mode-related group has exactly 1 member in the pressure table); the \\\"Iters: 3\\\" columns reflect recurring fuzzy-bucket labels (issue_hints, callers, all_locations are repeat categories across iterations, each surfacing distinct concerns), not the same concern being ignored three times. So TIEBREAKER threshold isn't met. Not ESCALATE either \u2014 trajectory is still productive and the remaining gaps are specific and fixable. **Fixes v4 must make**: (1) **Wire runner modes into the rendered entrypoint.** Replace the current idle-tmux boot with a mode switch at the end of `entrypoint.sh.tmpl`: when `${MODE}=auto`, the `agent` tmux session launches `megaplan auto --plan <derived-name> --idea ${IDEA_FILE}` (derive plan name from idea filename or require `auto.plan_name` in the spec); when `${MODE}=chain`, it launches `megaplan chain --spec ${CHAIN_SPEC}`; when `${MODE}=idle`, it drops to a shell (current behaviour). The spec's `auto.robustness` flows as `--robustness <value>` to `megaplan auto`. Port the existing `chain.sh` launcher into `megaplan/cloud/wrappers/mp-chain` (or inline into the entrypoint \u2014 either works, just pick one and name it) and confirm `chain.yaml.example` is documented in `docs/cloud.md`. Add a template test iterating over `mode \u2208 {auto, chain, idle}` and asserting the correct launch command appears in the rendered entrypoint. (2) **Fix the Dockerfile COPY path mismatch.** `materialize_deploy_dir()` flattens wrappers into the staging dir root, so the Dockerfile must either `COPY wrappers/* /usr/local/bin/` (simpler, matches staging) or `materialize_deploy_dir()` must preserve `megaplan/cloud/wrappers/` in the staging tree. Pick the first \u2014 it's cleaner. Add an assertion in the smoke `docker build` test that wrapper binaries end up on the image PATH (use `docker run --rm <image> which mp-run` or equivalent). This is the last clean iteration window: score is good and fixes are concrete, but if v4 still leaves mode-wiring out, the loop is oscillating on brief coverage and the next gate call is ESCALATE for user input on whether sprint 1 actually needs runtime modes vs. just the schema contract for sprint 2.",

The 7 open flags are tight fuzzy duplicates of just two underlying gaps (runner-mode wiring, Dockerfile COPY path), both concrete and fixable. The pressure-analysis \\\"Iters: 3\\\" on FG-001..FG-005 is misleading \u2014 it reflects recurring fuzzy-bucket labels across iterations rather than the same concern being ignored; within each iteration the specific bucketed flags (reigh-dir enumeration in iter1, workspace path in iter2, mode wiring in iter3) are different concerns. No debt-subsystem overlap. Not stuck.",

  "robustness": "standard",
  "signals": {
    "iteration": 3,
    "idea": "Build `megaplan cloud` \u2014 a new subpackage at `megaplan/cloud/` that provides a built-in, provider-agnostic cloud runner wired into the existing megaplan CLI. This is sprint 1 of a two-sprint effort; the goal here is operational parity with the existing Railway setup at `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/`, nothing more.\n\n### What exists today (to be generalised)\nThe folder `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/` contains a working but hardcoded Railway deployment. Read every file there during prep. Key pieces:\n- `Dockerfile` \u2014 Ubuntu 22.04 + pyenv/Python 3.11 + nvm/Node 20 + codex CLI + claude CLI + megaplan from git\n- `entrypoint.sh` \u2014 idempotent boot: codex auth (OPENAI_API_KEY), codex `config.toml` (model=gpt-5.4, reasoning=high, sandbox_mode=danger-full-access, approval_policy=never), megaplan install/upgrade from `MEGAPLAN_REF`, megaplan config (all phases\u2192codex, auto_approve=true, robustness=standard), repo clone, installs `mp-run`/`mp-supervise`/`mp-heartbeat` wrapper scripts (currently embedded as heredocs), starts tmux sessions `agent` and `heartbeat`, starts healthserver\n- `healthserver.py` \u2014 tiny HTTP server on :8080 for Railway healthcheck\n- `railway.toml` \u2014 build/deploy config, healthcheck, restart policy\n- `chain.sh` \u2014 thin shim around `megaplan chain --spec /workspace/chain.yaml`\n- `chain.yaml.example` \u2014 chain spec format\n- `README.md` \u2014 recipes for status/attach/resume/abort/restart/teardown that need to become subcommands\n\nHardcoded things to parameterise: `banodoco/reigh-app.git`, `/workspace/reigh-app`, `gpt-5.4` model, `high` reasoning, `[REDACTED]` email, all-phases\u2192codex routing, `standard` robustness. Bug to fix: Claude Code CLI is installed in the image but the entrypoint never authenticates it \u2014 add an `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` auth path.\n\n### Sprint 1 deliverables\n1. New subpackage `megaplan/cloud/` parallel to `megaplan/loop/` and `megaplan/chain.py`, with:\n   - Package resources: ported Dockerfile, entrypoint.sh, healthserver.py. Extract the mp-run/mp-supervise/mp-heartbeat bash scripts (currently embedded as heredocs in entrypoint.sh) into their own files under `megaplan/cloud/wrappers/`.\n   - Spec loader for `cloud.yaml` (schema covers at minimum: `provider`, `repo.url`/`branch`, `agents.default` + optional per-phase map, `codex.model` + `reasoning`, `mode` = auto|chain|idle, `auto.idea_file`, `auto.robustness`, `chain.spec`, `megaplan.ref`, `resources.volume`/`port`, `secrets` name list). PyYAML is not yet a dependency \u2014 confirm/add.\n   - Template renderer that materialises `entrypoint.sh` with spec values at deploy time, so the baked docker image stays generic while the per-deploy entrypoint carries the config.\n   - Claude auth path in the rendered entrypoint: if `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` env is set, run non-interactive auth (investigate what `claude` CLI exposes for this \u2014 `claude setup-token` or similar; document whatever you find).\n   - Railway provider plugin: thin wrapper over `railway` CLI (`up`, `down`, `ssh`, `variables --set`, `logs`, `status`). Live at `megaplan/cloud/providers/railway.py` with a `Provider` abstract base at `megaplan/cloud/providers/base.py` ready for sprint-2 plugins.\n\n2. New argparse subcommands wired into `megaplan/cli.py`:\n   - `megaplan cloud init` \u2014 scaffold `cloud.yaml` at project root\n   - `megaplan cloud build` \u2014 docker build the image\n   - `megaplan cloud deploy` \u2014 render entrypoint, upload vars, `railway up --detach --ci`\n   - `megaplan cloud status` \u2014 invoke `megaplan status` remotely via provider ssh, return structured JSON (do NOT reinvent a parser \u2014 reuse what `megaplan status` already emits)\n   - `megaplan cloud attach` \u2014 attach to tmux `agent` session\n   - `megaplan cloud logs` \u2014 tail remote logs\n   - `megaplan cloud exec \"<cmd>\"` \u2014 run arbitrary remote command (replaces README one-liners)\n   - `megaplan cloud resume` \u2014 read remote plan state, dispatch `next_step`\n   - `megaplan cloud down` \u2014 pause without deleting volume\n   - `megaplan cloud destroy` \u2014 full teardown\n\n3. Documentation at `docs/cloud.md`: env-var / cloud.yaml reference, symptom\u2192cause\u2192fix table (port from existing README), secret-rotation and cost-monitoring pointers.\n\n4. Tests:\n   - Unit: spec loader (happy path + validation errors)\n   - Unit: template renderer (all hardcoded strings correctly replaced)\n   - Unit: Railway provider with mocked subprocess\n   - Smoke: `docker build` on the generated Dockerfile must succeed (CI-runnable; can be skipped on systems without docker with a clear warning)\n\n5. Reigh parity validation (success criterion, not a test to automate): after implementation, the existing `reigh-megaplan-dev` behavior can be reproduced by `megaplan cloud deploy` with a `cloud.yaml` matching reigh's current env vars. The validation itself can be deferred to a follow-up runbook; the code must make it possible.\n\n### Explicit non-goals for sprint 1\n- Do NOT ship fly / ssh / local providers \u2014 that is sprint 2.\n- Do NOT ship `cloud init-plan` bootstrap \u2014 sprint 2.\n- Do NOT add a `toolchains:` block in `cloud.yaml` \u2014 sprint 2.\n- Do NOT retire `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/` \u2014 it keeps running in parallel until sprint 2 migrates reigh fully.\n\n### Constraints (hard requirements)\n- Sprint 1 must be independently shippable: if sprint 2 slips, this release is still useful on its own.\n- Preserve the idempotency contract of the current entrypoint (repo clone only if missing; codex config always overwritten on boot; megaplan always upgraded on boot).\n- Preserve `sandbox_mode = \"danger-full-access\"` in the generated codex config with an explanatory comment \u2014 the container IS the sandbox.\n- Preserve the `MEGAPLAN_REF` pinning mechanism, now surfaced as `megaplan.ref` in `cloud.yaml` (defaults to `main`).\n- `railway` CLI is an acceptable runtime dependency; document it and fail gracefully with a link if missing.\n- Do not break existing megaplan commands \u2014 `cloud` is additive.\n\n### References\n- Existing source at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/` \u2014 read everything.\n- Target subpackage location: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/megaplan/megaplan/cloud/`.\n- Current CLI wiring pattern: `megaplan/cli.py` uses argparse with `subparsers.add_parser(...)` (see lines ~830-1005).\n- Verified launcher for megaplan commands: `./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan ...`. Bare `python` resolves to 3.8.10 which fails on `NotRequired`; the repo venv has 3.11.",

        "concern": "Cloud runtime: remote `status` is planned around `./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan`, but the deployed container installs megaplan globally and the existing Railway workflow uses plain `megaplan` inside `/workspace/reigh-app`.",
        "resolution": "Addressed every open significant flag without redesigning the architecture. Concrete corrections: (1) remote commands now use bare `megaplan` (image installs it globally) instead of `./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan`; (2) renderer emits `megaplan config set agents.<step> <agent>` validated against `DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING` \u2014 fabricated `phase_agent.*` namespace is gone; (3) new `cloud resume` step explicitly imports and reuses `megaplan.auto._phase_command()` so the `execute` special case (`--confirm-destructive --user-approved`) cannot silently break, with a new dedicated test (`tests/test_cloud_resume.py`); (4) added a `railway:` nested block in `cloud.yaml` carrying `service`/`session`/`project`, threaded through every railway invocation, kept under a per-provider namespace so sprint-2 providers stay clean; (5) Step 1 now reads every file in the reigh dir including `BRIEFING_RULES.md`, `ideas/`, `megaplan-idea*.txt`; (6) dropped the duplicate remote active-plan lookup \u2014 `cloud status`/`cloud resume` simply pass `--plan` through (or omit) and let `load_plan(root, None)` resolve server-side; (7) phase validation now imports `DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING` from `megaplan.types` instead of hand-maintaining a phase set, removing drift risk. Added a Step 0 vendoring path for sandboxed executors (per orchestrator warning), and committed the rendered Claude-auth block to \"warn, do not block\" semantics (per user note #3).         "id": "correctness-1",
        "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: Cloud config: the revised plan still does not provide a schema field for the repo checkout path even though reigh hardcodes `REPO_DIR=/workspace/reigh-app`, sets git `safe.directory` to that path, starts tmux in that directory, and the README's status/resume commands all `cd` there. Because `render_entrypoint()`, `status_payload()`, and `resume` now all depend on `WORKSPACE_PATH`, the lack of a declared source of truth would force the executor to invent a derivation rule that is not specified in the plan.",
        "resolution": "All 10 open flags collapse into 4 mechanical fixes; none required redesign. (1) Workspace path: added `repo.workspace: str` field (default `/workspace/app`, validated absolute), threaded through `template.PLACEHOLDERS[\"WORKSPACE_PATH\"]` (every reigh `/workspace/reigh-app` site), `Provider.status_payload(workspace=...)`, and `cloud resume`'s remote command construction. New template test asserts a non-default workspace flows end-to-end. Closes issue_hints, correctness-1, callers-1, FLAG-005. (2) Volume teardown: extended `Provider.destroy(*, volume=None)`; `RailwayProvider.destroy` runs `railway down` then `railway volume delete <volume>` when set, surfacing failures as `CliError`. CLI prompt names the volume. New tests cover both `destroy(volume=None)` and `destroy(volume=\"agent-volume\")` plus the failure path. Closes correctness-2, callers-2, FLAG-006, all_locations-1. (3) `railway.project`: now wired \u2014 `RailwayProvider.deploy` calls `railway link --project <project>` once before any `variables`/`up` calls when set; new test asserts the call sequence. No more dead knob. Closes scope. (4) `_reference/` plumbing: Step 0 explicitly appends the path to `.gitignore` and adds a `[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel.exclude]` entry to `pyproject.toml`; validation step 6 confirms the wheel doesn't ship it. Closes all_locations-2. Also: `docs/cloud.md` now documents the workspace field, volume-delete semantics in `cloud destroy`, and the recommended `cloud exec` pattern (does NOT auto-cd)."
      },

        Requirements:
        - Before addressing individual flags, check: does any flag suggest the plan is targeting the wrong code or the wrong root cause? If so, consider whether the plan needs a new approach rather than adjustments. Explain your reasoning.
        - Update the plan to address the significant issues.
        - Keep the plan readable and executable.
        - Return flags_addressed with the exact flag IDs you addressed.
        - Include `changes_summary` as a short plain-English summary of what changed in the revision. If there were no concrete flags, say that explicitly (for example: `No critique flags were raised; refined wording and kept the plan aligned for execution.`).
        - Preserve or improve success criteria quality. Each criterion must have a `priority` of `must`, `should`, or `info`. Promote or demote priorities if critique feedback reveals a criterion was over- or under-weighted.
        - Verify that the plan remains aligned with the user's original intent, not just internal plan quality.
        - Remove unjustified scope growth. If critique raised scope creep, narrow the plan back to the original idea unless the broader work is strictly required.
        - Maintain the structural template: H1 title, ## Overview, phase sections with numbered step sections, ## Execution Order or ## Validation Order.
        - CRITICAL: Your entire revised plan markdown (all sections) must be output as the `plan` field in the structured output. The prose response must not contain the plan text.
        - CRITICAL: Return only the structured JSON object for the schema fields `plan`, `changes_summary`, `flags_addressed`, `assumptions`, `success_criteria`, and `questions`. Do not add commentary before or after the JSON object.

        Plan template — simple format (adapt to the actual repo and scope):
````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

use bare `megaplan ...`, NOT `./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan`.\n- **Config namespace**: `megaplan config set` only accepts `agents.<step>` validated against `DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING` (`cli.py:758-777`, `types.py:296-309`).\n- **`next_step` translation**: `megaplan/auto.py:128-136` (`_phase_command`) is the canonical translator; `cloud resume` reuses it.\n- **Active-plan resolution**: `handle_status` (`cli.py:353-367`) auto-resolves when `args.plan is None`; cloud passes `--plan` through if supplied, omits otherwise.\n- **Railway service/session targeting**: every railway invocation includes `--service <service>`; ssh/attach include `--session <session>`.\n- **Workspace path is data**: `repo.workspace` carries the container checkout path; templates and remote commands read from it.\n- **Volume teardown is part of `destroy`**: `Provider.destroy(*, volume=None)` honors a configured volume.\n- **`megaplan auto` requires `--plan <name>`** (`auto.py:489`): mode=auto needs an explicit plan name in the spec OR the entrypoint must bootstrap one via `megaplan init` first.\n- **No dead knobs**: `railway.project` wires to `railway link --project <project>` at deploy time when set.\n- **Docker build context is the staged deploy dir**, not the repo root: the Dockerfile's `COPY` paths must match what `materialize_deploy_dir()` actually writes into the staging dir.\n\nOther constraints:\n- Sprint 1 must be independently shippable.\n- Idempotency contract preserved (clone-if-missing, codex config always overwritten, megaplan always upgraded).\n- Codex `sandbox_mode = \"danger-full-access\"` retained with explanatory comment.\n- `MEGAPLAN_REF` pin surfaced as `megaplan.ref` (default `main`).\n- Adding `cloud` must not break any existing command.\n- Reigh parity: a `cloud.yaml` matching today's reigh env vars must reproduce the existing deployment, including unattended `auto`/`chain` runner modes (not just an idle shell).\n\n## Phase 1: Reconcile Source & Scaffold Subpackage\n\n### Step 0: Vendor reigh source as reference, with ignore + build exclusion (`megaplan/cloud/_reference/`, `.gitignore`, `pyproject.toml`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Verify** the executor can read `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/`. If readable, proceed; if sandboxed, copy the entire directory tree into `megaplan/cloud/_reference/`.\n2. **Append** `megaplan/cloud/_reference/` to `.gitignore`.\n3. **Exclude** the snapshot from packaged artifacts: add `[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel.exclude] = [\"megaplan/cloud/_reference/**\"]` (or equivalent) to `pyproject.toml`. Verify by inspecting the built wheel — `_reference/` must NOT appear.\n4. **Document** the snapshot status in `megaplan/cloud/_reference/README.md`.\n\n### Step 1: Read every file in the reigh deployment\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Read** every file in `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/` (or the vendored mirror): `Dockerfile`, `entrypoint.sh`, `healthserver.py`, `railway.toml`, `chain.sh`, `chain.yaml.example`, `README.md`, `BRIEFING_RULES.md`, every file under `ideas/`, every top-level `megaplan-idea*.txt`, plus anything else present.\n2. **Catalogue** every hardcoded value (repo URL, **workspace path** `/workspace/reigh-app`, model, reasoning, email, agent routing, robustness, **persistent volume name** `agent-volume`) and every env var referenced.\n3. **Capture** the exact `chain.sh` body (per the brief: \"thin shim around `megaplan chain --spec /workspace/chain.yaml`\") so Step 3 can either inline it into the entrypoint or write it as a wrapper.\n4. **Note** the exact mp-run / mp-supervise / mp-heartbeat heredoc bodies — these become standalone files in step 3.\n5. **Identify** the exact `claude` CLI auth invocation. If no non-interactive path exists, the rendered entrypoint emits a warning and continues (do NOT block deploy).\n6. **Note** how reigh starts its `agent` tmux session today — specifically what command runs inside it (idle shell, `megaplan auto ...`, or `chain.sh`). This determines the default mode mapping for Step 3.\n\n### Step 2: Scaffold the subpackage layout (`megaplan/cloud/`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Create** the directory tree:\n   ```\n   megaplan/cloud/\n     __init__.py\n     cli.py              # build_cloud_parser + run_cloud_cli\n     spec.py             # cloud.yaml dataclasses + loader/validator\n     template.py         # entrypoint renderer + materialize_deploy_dir\n     providers/\n       __init__.py\n       base.py           # Provider ABC\n       railway.py        # Railway implementation\n     templates/\n       Dockerfile\n       entrypoint.sh.tmpl\n       healthserver.py\n       railway.toml.tmpl\n       cloud.yaml.tmpl\n     wrappers/\n       mp-run\n       mp-supervise\n       mp-heartbeat\n       mp-chain          # one-line shim: exec megaplan chain --spec \"$1\"\n   ```\n2. **Confirm** hatchling packages the non-`.py` resources by default. Add `force-include` if needed.\n3. **Verify** by building the wheel locally and inspecting contents — `templates/` and `wrappers/` present; `_reference/` absent.\n\n### Step 3: Port resources, parameterise, and add the mode switch (`megaplan/cloud/templates/`, `megaplan/cloud/wrappers/`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Copy** `Dockerfile`, `healthserver.py`, and `railway.toml` from reigh into `megaplan/cloud/templates/`.\n2. **Rewrite** the Dockerfile's `COPY` lines to match the staging-dir layout produced by `materialize_deploy_dir()` (Step 5): the build context root contains `entrypoint.sh`, `healthserver.py`, `railway.toml`, AND a `wrappers/` subdir. So the Dockerfile uses:\n   ```dockerfile\n   COPY entrypoint.sh /usr/local/bin/entrypoint.sh\n   COPY healthserver.py /usr/local/bin/healthserver.py\n   COPY wrappers/ /usr/local/bin/\n   RUN chmod +x /usr/local/bin/entrypoint.sh /usr/local/bin/mp-* \n   ```\n   No `megaplan/cloud/wrappers/...` paths anywhere — those don't exist in the build context. (Closes FLAG-007 / correctness.)\n3. **Copy** `entrypoint.sh` to `megaplan/cloud/templates/entrypoint.sh.tmpl` and replace every hardcoded value with a `string.Template` placeholder. Complete placeholder list:\n   - `${REPO_URL}`, `${REPO_BRANCH}`\n   - `${WORKSPACE_PATH}` — used in 5 places: git `safe.directory`, `REPO_DIR=`, the `git clone` destination, the tmux `agent` session `-c <path>`, and any other `cd` reference.\n   - `${CODEX_MODEL}`, `${CODEX_REASONING}`, `${CODEX_EMAIL}`\n   - `${MEGAPLAN_REF}`, `${ROBUSTNESS}`\n   - `${MODE}`, `${IDEA_FILE}`, `${CHAIN_SPEC}`, `${AUTO_PLAN_NAME}`\n   - `${AGENT_ROUTING_BLOCK}` (multi-line `megaplan config set agents.<step> <agent>` lines)\n   - `${CLAUDE_AUTH_BLOCK}` (the gated auth-or-warn shell block)\n   - `${RUNNER_LAUNCH_BLOCK}` — see step 3.5.\n   Keep the explanatory comment for `sandbox_mode = \"danger-full-access\"`.\n4. **Render** the Claude auth block (warn-and-continue, never `exit 1`).\n5. **Add the mode switch as `${RUNNER_LAUNCH_BLOCK}`** — this is what makes the cloud layer a runner, not just an idle container. The renderer (Step 5) builds this block from `spec.mode`. The substituted shell looks like:\n   - `mode = \"auto\"`:\n     ```sh\n     # Bootstrap plan if it doesn't exist; then drive it.\n     PLAN_NAME=\"${AUTO_PLAN_NAME}\"\n     if [ ! -d \"${WORKSPACE_PATH}/.megaplan/plans/$PLAN_NAME\" ]; then\n         (cd \"${WORKSPACE_PATH}\" && megaplan init --project-dir \"${WORKSPACE_PATH}\" --name \"$PLAN_NAME\" --robustness \"${ROBUSTNESS}\" \"$(cat ${IDEA_FILE})\")\n     fi\n     tmux new-session -d -s agent -c \"${WORKSPACE_PATH}\" \"megaplan auto --plan $PLAN_NAME\"\n     ```\n   - `mode = \"chain\"`:\n     ```sh\n     tmux new-session -d -s agent -c \"${WORKSPACE_PATH}\" \"mp-chain ${CHAIN_SPEC}\"\n     ```\n     where `mp-chain` is the wrapper installed in Step 2 (`exec megaplan chain --spec \"$1\"`).\n   - `mode = \"idle\"`:\n     ```sh\n     tmux new-session -d -s agent -c \"${WORKSPACE_PATH}\" \"bash\"\n     ```\n   The `heartbeat` tmux session and `healthserver` always start regardless of mode (they don't depend on the runner). The block is one of three string literals chosen by the renderer, NOT a `case` in the template — keeps the rendered shell free of dead branches.\n6. **Extract** the mp-run / mp-supervise / mp-heartbeat heredocs into standalone files under `megaplan/cloud/wrappers/`. Add `mp-chain` (one-line shim).\n7. **Add** `cloud.yaml.tmpl` with sensible defaults filled in (including `repo.workspace: /workspace/app`, `mode: idle`, `auto: { plan_name: my-plan, idea_file: /workspace/idea.txt, robustness: standard }`, `chain: { spec: /workspace/chain.yaml }`, `railway.service: agent`, `railway.session: agent`, `resources.volume: agent-volume`) and inline comments explaining each section, INCLUDING what `mode` does to the runner.\n8. **Add** `chain.yaml.example` to `megaplan/cloud/templates/` (port from reigh) so users have a starting point referenced by `docs/cloud.md`.\n\n### Step 4: Spec loader (`megaplan/cloud/spec.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Define** dataclasses. Note `auto.plan_name` is now required when `mode=auto` (the runner needs `--plan <name>`):\n   ```python\n   @dataclass\n   class RepoSpec:\n       url: str\n       branch: str = \"main\"\n       workspace: str = \"/workspace/app\"\n   @dataclass\n   class CodexSpec: model: str = \"gpt-5.4\"; reasoning: str = \"high\"\n   @dataclass\n   class AutoSpec:\n       plan_name: str                       # required: passed as `megaplan auto --plan <name>`\n       idea_file: str                       # absolute container path; passed to `megaplan init` if plan doesn't exist\n       robustness: str = \"standard\"\n   @dataclass\n   class ChainSubSpec: spec: str            # absolute container path to chain YAML\n   @dataclass\n   class ResourcesSpec: volume: str | None = None; port: int = 8080\n   @dataclass\n   class MegaplanSpec: ref: str = \"main\"\n   @dataclass\n   class RailwaySpec:\n       service: str = \"agent\"\n       session: str = \"agent\"\n       project: str | None = None\n   @dataclass\n   class CloudSpec:\n       provider: str\n       repo: RepoSpec\n       agents: dict[str, str]\n       codex: CodexSpec\n       mode: str                            # \"auto\" | \"chain\" | \"idle\"\n       megaplan: MegaplanSpec\n       resources: ResourcesSpec\n       secrets: list[str]\n       auto: AutoSpec | None = None\n       chain: ChainSubSpec | None = None\n       railway: RailwaySpec | None = None\n   ```\n2. **Implement** `load_spec(path: Path) -> CloudSpec` — open, `yaml.safe_load`, validate, raise `CliError(\"invalid_spec\", ...)` on every missing/malformed field.\n3. **Validate** mode-specific requirements:\n   - `mode=auto` ⇒ `auto.plan_name` AND `auto.idea_file` BOTH required (and `auto.idea_file` must be absolute).\n   - `mode=chain` ⇒ `chain.spec` required (and absolute).\n   - `mode=idle` ⇒ neither auto nor chain required.\n4. **Validate** `mode` ∈ `{\"auto\", \"chain\", \"idle\"}`; reject anything else with a `CliError` that names the valid set.\n5. **Validate** `agents` keys: `default` permitted; every other key MUST be in `DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING` (imported from `megaplan.types`). Reject unknown keys naming the actual valid set.\n6. **Validate** every value in `agents` is in `KNOWN_AGENTS`.\n7. **Validate** `provider`: only `\"railway\"` permitted in sprint 1; reject others with `\"sprint 2 will add: fly, ssh, local\"`.\n8. **Validate** `repo.workspace` is an absolute POSIX path.\n9. **Fill** `RailwaySpec` with defaults if the `railway:` block is omitted.\n\n### Step 5: Template renderer (`megaplan/cloud/template.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Implement** `render_entrypoint(spec: CloudSpec) -> str` using `string.Template.safe_substitute`. The substitution map MUST include `WORKSPACE_PATH=spec.repo.workspace` and `RUNNER_LAUNCH_BLOCK=_runner_block(spec)`, where `_runner_block` returns the appropriate auto/chain/idle shell snippet from Step 3.5 with all `${...}` already substituted (the renderer does double-substitution: build the block first, substitute its values, then drop it into the parent template).\n2. **Build** the agent-routing block. For each entry in `spec.agents`:\n   - `default` → `megaplan config set agents.<step> <default_agent>` for every `<step>` in `DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING`.\n   - Per-step keys → `megaplan config set agents.<step> <agent>` (overrides default).\n3. **Implement** `materialize_deploy_dir(spec, dest: Path)` that lays out the Docker build context as:\n   ```\n   <dest>/\n     Dockerfile          # untemplated copy\n     entrypoint.sh       # rendered from entrypoint.sh.tmpl\n     healthserver.py     # untemplated copy\n     railway.toml        # rendered from railway.toml.tmpl\n     wrappers/\n       mp-run\n       mp-supervise\n       mp-heartbeat\n       mp-chain\n   ```\n   This layout is what the Dockerfile in Step 3.2 references (`COPY wrappers/ /usr/local/bin/`). Mismatch between this layout and the Dockerfile's COPY paths is the FLAG-007 regression — the renderer test in Step 10 asserts the layout.\n4. **Single source of truth**: define placeholder names as a module-level tuple `PLACEHOLDERS = (\"REPO_URL\", \"REPO_BRANCH\", \"WORKSPACE_PATH\", \"MODE\", \"AUTO_PLAN_NAME\", \"IDEA_FILE\", \"CHAIN_SPEC\", ...)` so tests iterate over them.\n\n## Phase 2: Provider Layer\n\n### Step 6: Provider abstract base (`megaplan/cloud/providers/base.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Define** an `abc.ABC` `Provider`:\n   ```python\n   class Provider(ABC):\n       def build(self, deploy_dir: Path) -> int: ...\n       def deploy(self, deploy_dir: Path, *, secrets: dict[str, str]) -> int: ...\n       def ssh_exec(self, command: str) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess: ...\n       def attach(self) -> int: ...\n       def logs(self, *, follow: bool = True) -> int: ...\n       def status_payload(self, *, plan: str | None, workspace: str) -> dict: ...\n       def down(self) -> int: ...\n       def destroy(self, *, volume: str | None = None) -> int: ...\n   ```\n2. **Document** that sprint 2 will add `init_plan(self, ...)` and providers must remain stateless beyond CLI/credential discovery.\n3. **Provide** `get_provider(name: str, spec: CloudSpec) -> Provider`. Static dict for sprint 1: `{\"railway\": RailwayProvider}`.\n\n### Step 7: Railway provider (`megaplan/cloud/providers/railway.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Construct** with `RailwaySpec` and stash `repo.workspace`.\n2. **Implement** each ABC method:\n   - `build`: `docker build -t <image-tag> <deploy_dir>`.\n   - `deploy(deploy_dir, *, secrets)`:\n     1. If `spec.railway.project is not None`: run `railway link --project <project>` once.\n     2. For each `(name, value)` in `secrets`: `railway variables --service <service> --set NAME=VALUE`.\n     3. `railway up --service <service> --detach --ci` from the deploy dir.\n   - `ssh_exec(cmd)`: `railway ssh --service <service> --session <session> -- <cmd>`.\n   - `attach`: `railway ssh --service <service> --session <session>` (interactive).\n   - `logs`: `railway logs --service <service>` (with `--follow` if requested).\n   - `status_payload(plan, workspace)`: remote `cd <workspace> && megaplan status [--plan <plan>]`. Stream stdout, parse JSON, return.\n   - `down`: `railway down --service <service>`.\n   - `destroy(*, volume)`: `railway down --service <service>`; if `volume` is set, then `railway volume delete <volume>` (non-zero exit raises `CliError(\"provider_failed\", stderr)`).\n3. **Detect** the `railway` binary up front; missing ⇒ `CliError(\"provider_unavailable\", \"Install: https://docs.railway.app/develop/cli\")`.\n4. **`deploy()` secrets**: source from `os.environ`; missing ⇒ `CliError(\"missing_secrets\", ...)` BEFORE any railway calls.\n5. **No retry/backoff**; surface non-zero exits as `CliError(\"provider_failed\", stderr)`.\n\n## Phase 3: CLI Wiring\n\n### Step 8: Cloud CLI module (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Implement** `build_cloud_parser(subparsers)` modelled on `chain.py:528-555`. Subparsers: `init`, `build`, `deploy`, `status`, `attach`, `logs`, `exec`, `resume`, `down`, `destroy`. All accept `--cloud-yaml PATH` (default `<root>/cloud.yaml`).\n2. **Implement** `run_cloud_cli(root, args) -> int`.\n3. **Per-action behaviours:**\n   - **`init`** — copies `cloud.yaml.tmpl` to `<root>/cloud.yaml`. Refuses to overwrite without `--force`.\n   - **`build`** — renders deploy dir via `materialize_deploy_dir`, calls `provider.build(deploy_dir)`.\n   - **`deploy`** — renders deploy dir, gathers secrets, calls `provider.deploy(...)`.\n   - **`status`** — accepts optional `--plan`; calls `provider.status_payload(plan=args.plan, workspace=spec.repo.workspace)`; prints JSON.\n   - **`attach`** — calls `provider.attach()`. `--session NAME` overrides the spec default.\n   - **`logs`** — calls `provider.logs(follow=True)`. `--no-follow` for one-shot.\n   - **`exec \"<cmd>\"`** — calls `provider.ssh_exec(cmd)`. Does NOT auto-prepend `cd <workspace>`.\n   - **`resume`** — accepts optional `--plan`. Steps:\n     1. `provider.status_payload(plan=args.plan, workspace=spec.repo.workspace)`.\n     2. `from megaplan.auto import _phase_command; argv = _phase_command(next_step)`.\n     3. Remote: `cd <workspace> && megaplan ` + shell-quoted `argv` + (`--plan <plan>` if user supplied).\n     4. `provider.ssh_exec(...)`.\n   - **`down`** — `provider.down()`.\n   - **`destroy`** — interactive confirmation by default (prompt names the volume); `--yes` skips. Calls `provider.destroy(volume=spec.resources.volume)`.\n4. **Errors:** `CliError`s flow through `_emit_error` (mirror `chain.py:594-597`).\n\n### Step 9: Wire into top-level CLI (`megaplan/cli.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Register** parser inside `build_parser()` next to `build_chain_parser` (`cli.py:997-998`).\n2. **Dispatch** in `main()` next to the `chain` block (`cli.py:1133-1138`).\n3. **Do NOT** add `\"cloud\"` to `COMMAND_HANDLERS`.\n4. **Keep** imports lazy/scoped.\n\n## Phase 4: Tests, Docs, Smoke Build\n\n### Step 10: Unit tests for spec & renderer (`tests/test_cloud_spec.py`, `tests/test_cloud_template.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **`test_cloud_spec.py`** — happy-path load for each `mode`, plus rejections: missing `repo.url`, `repo.workspace` non-absolute, unknown `mode`, **`mode=auto` without `auto.plan_name`**, **`mode=auto` without `auto.idea_file`**, **`mode=auto` with non-absolute `idea_file`**, `mode=chain` without `chain.spec`, `mode=chain` with non-absolute `chain.spec`, unknown provider, unknown `agents` key (asserts error names valid set), unknown agent value, malformed `secrets`, missing `railway:` block (filled with defaults).\n2. **`test_cloud_template.py`** — render with a fully-populated spec for each mode. Assert:\n   - (a) every documented placeholder is replaced (iterate over `template.PLACEHOLDERS`).\n   - (b) no original reigh hardcoded string remains (`banodoco/reigh-app`, `gpt-5.4`, `[REDACTED]`, `/workspace/reigh-app`).\n   - (c) `sandbox_mode = \"danger-full-access\"` line preserved.\n   - (d) Claude auth block present, gated on `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, warn-not-fatal.\n   - (e) agent-routing block emits ONLY `megaplan config set agents.<step> <agent>` lines.\n   - (f) per-step override emits exactly one overridden `agents.<step>` line.\n   - (g) non-default `repo.workspace` flows into every `WORKSPACE_PATH` site.\n   - (h) **mode-switch correctness** (one assertion per mode):\n       - `mode=auto`: rendered entrypoint contains `megaplan auto --plan <plan_name>` AND a fallback `megaplan init --project-dir <workspace> --name <plan_name>` block; tmux `new-session ... -c <workspace>` references the workspace.\n       - `mode=chain`: rendered entrypoint contains `mp-chain <chain.spec>` (or `megaplan chain --spec <chain.spec>`).\n       - `mode=idle`: rendered entrypoint launches `bash` (no `megaplan auto` or `megaplan chain` reference).\n3. **`test_cloud_template.py` (cont.)** — `materialize_deploy_dir`: assert the staging dir has `Dockerfile`, `entrypoint.sh`, `healthserver.py`, `railway.toml` at root AND a `wrappers/` subdir containing `mp-run`, `mp-supervise`, `mp-heartbeat`, `mp-chain`. Assert the rendered `Dockerfile` contains `COPY wrappers/ /usr/local/bin/` (and does NOT contain `megaplan/cloud/wrappers`).\n\n### Step 11: Unit test for the Railway provider with mocked subprocess (`tests/test_cloud_railway.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Patch** `subprocess.run` and `shutil.which`; assert exact `railway ...` argv per method.\n2. **Cover** `--service <service>` on every call; `--session <session>` on `ssh_exec`/`attach`.\n3. **Cover** `build`, `deploy` (per-secret upload sequence; when `spec.railway.project` is set assert `railway link --project <project>` is called exactly once before any `variables`/`up`), `down`, `ssh_exec`, `attach`, `logs`.\n4. **`status_payload(plan=None, workspace=\"/workspace/foo\")`** — remote command is `cd /workspace/foo && megaplan status`; with `plan=\"P\"`, `cd /workspace/foo && megaplan status --plan P`.\n5. **`destroy(volume=None)`** — exactly one `railway down --service <service>` call.\n6. **`destroy(volume=\"agent-volume\")`** — `railway down --service <service>` then `railway volume delete agent-volume`. Add a failure-path test where the volume-delete returns non-zero and assert `CliError(\"provider_failed\", ...)`.\n7. **Cover** missing-binary path: `shutil.which(\"railway\") -> None` ⇒ `CliError(\"provider_unavailable\", ...)`.\n8. **Cover** missing-secret path: `CliError` with missing names BEFORE any railway calls.\n\n### Step 12: Unit test for `cloud resume` translation (`tests/test_cloud_resume.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Mock** `provider.status_payload` returning `{\"next_step\": \"execute\"}`. Assert `provider.ssh_exec` called with a remote command containing `cd <workspace> && megaplan execute --confirm-destructive --user-approved`.\n2. **Cover** `next_step == \"review\"` (bare-name path).\n3. **Assert** `--plan <name>` appended when user passed `--plan`, omitted otherwise.\n4. **Assert** `cd` target is `spec.repo.workspace` (not hardcoded).\n\n### Step 13: Smoke test for docker build (`tests/test_cloud_docker_build.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Skip** at module import if `shutil.which(\"docker\") is None`.\n2. **Materialise** a fully-rendered deploy dir into `tmp_path` via `materialize_deploy_dir`, run `docker build -t megaplan-cloud-smoke .` against it. Fail on non-zero exit. (This test would have caught FLAG-007 directly; it's the regression guard for the COPY-path fix.)\n3. **Assert** wrapper binaries reach the image PATH: `docker run --rm megaplan-cloud-smoke which mp-run` returns 0.\n4. **Mark** with `@pytest.mark.slow`.\n\n### Step 14: User docs (`docs/cloud.md`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Create** `docs/cloud.md`. Sections: quick start, `cloud.yaml` field reference (including `repo.workspace`, the `railway:` block with `service`/`session`/`project`, `resources.volume`, AND the `mode` field with explicit per-mode behavior table — what gets launched in the `agent` tmux session for each mode and which `auto.*`/`chain.*` fields each requires), env vars, secret-rotation pointer, cost-monitoring pointer (Railway dashboard link), the symptom→cause→fix table.\n2. **Document** the runner-mode switch with concrete examples:\n   - `mode: auto` → entrypoint runs `megaplan init` (if needed) then `megaplan auto --plan <auto.plan_name>` in the `agent` tmux session.\n   - `mode: chain` → entrypoint runs `mp-chain <chain.spec>` (which is `megaplan chain --spec ...`) in the `agent` tmux session. Reference the bundled `chain.yaml.example` from Step 3.8.\n   - `mode: idle` → entrypoint drops to a `bash` shell; user drives manually via `cloud exec` / `cloud attach`.\n3. **Document** the resolved Claude non-interactive auth path (and warn-not-fatal behaviour).\n4. **Document** `cloud destroy` semantics: `railway down --service <service>` then, if `resources.volume` is set, `railway volume delete <volume>`.\n5. **Document** `cloud exec` does NOT auto-`cd`; recommended pattern: `megaplan cloud exec \"cd /workspace/app && megaplan ...\"`.\n6. **Document** the `railway` CLI install link.\n7. **Brief mention** that sprint 2 will add fly/ssh/local providers and `cloud init-plan` (a friendlier bootstrap than the current \"set `auto.plan_name` yourself\" path).\n\n## Execution Order\n1. Steps 0–1 first (vendoring + full reigh read).\n2. Steps 2–3 lay down resources before any code references them — Step 3 wires the runner-mode switch into the template AND fixes the Dockerfile COPY paths to match the staging dir.\n3. Steps 4–5 (spec + renderer) before steps 6–7 (providers depend on spec dataclasses including `auto.plan_name` and `repo.workspace`).\n4. Steps 8–9 (CLI wiring) after providers exist.\n5. Tests (10–13) run alongside or after each implementation step they cover; the docker smoke test (Step 13) is the regression guard for the COPY-path fix.\n6. Docs (14) last.\n\n## Validation Order\n1. Run `pytest tests/test_cloud_spec.py tests/test_cloud_template.py tests/test_cloud_resume.py -x`.\n2. Run `pytest tests/test_cloud_railway.py -x`.\n3. Run the full suite: `./.venv/bin/python -m pytest -x` to confirm no regression.\n4. Run `./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan cloud init` in a scratch dir; confirm `cloud.yaml` is written with `repo.workspace`, `mode`, `auto.plan_name`, `railway.service`, `resources.volume` defaults.\n5. Run `./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan cloud build` against the scratch `cloud.yaml`; confirm a Docker image is produced — the build context layout matches the Dockerfile COPY paths (skip if no docker).\n6. Build the wheel; confirm `_reference/` absent, `templates/` and `wrappers/` present.\n7. 1. **Runner modes wired end-to-end** (closes `issue_hints`, `all_locations`, `scope`, `FLAG-008`, `callers`): added `auto.plan_name` to the schema (required when `mode=auto`, since `auto.py:489` requires `--plan`); added `${RUNNER_LAUNCH_BLOCK}` to the entrypoint template; renderer chooses one of three concrete shell snippets (`auto` runs `megaplan init` if needed then `megaplan auto --plan <name>` in the `agent` tmux; `chain` runs `mp-chain <spec>`; `idle` drops to bash); ported `chain.yaml.example`; added `mp-chain` wrapper. 2. **Dockerfile COPY paths fixed** (closes `FLAG-007`, `correctness`): Dockerfile now uses `COPY wrappers/ /usr/local/bin/` matching the staged build context; `materialize_deploy_dir()` is explicitly specified to lay out `wrappers/` as a subdir of the staging dir; new template test asserts the layout matches; smoke `docker build` test now also runs `docker run --rm <image> which mp-run` as a direct regression guard.
